Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the stochastic_analysis structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 788. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 331 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the stochastic_analysis decomposition. By minimality, the element a^788 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 331-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.
